Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The spin operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 350. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 881 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to spin structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 350/881 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on spin structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 779. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 474 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 779 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the spin structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 474. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
